# RMS — Restaurant Management System — Worklog

Single-page app on `/` (src/app/page.tsx) with client-side view switching.
Stack: Next.js 16 App Router + TS, Tailwind 4 + shadcn/ui, Prisma + SQLite, TanStack Query (polling), JWT auth (jose) in httpOnly cookie `rms_session`, bcryptjs passwords.

## Global conventions (ALL agents MUST follow)

- **Currency/time**: use `@/lib/format` (formatCurrency EGP, formatTime, elapsedSince…).
- **Constants**: `@/lib/constants` (TAX_RATE=0.14, COURSES, PAYMENT_METHODS, ITEM_STATUSES, TABLE_STATUSES, INVENTORY_REASONS, RESTAURANT_NAME='Saffron Table', ROLE_LABELS).
- **Types**: `@/lib/types` (Product, Order, OrderItem, Payment, RestaurantTable, FloorPlan, Category, SessionUser, RecipeComponent, InventoryItem, InventoryTransaction, SalesReport…).
- **Server**: `import { db } from '@/lib/db'`; auth guard: `requireAuth(req, roles?)`, errors: `errorResponse(err)` / `throw new ApiError(msg, status)` from `@/lib/auth`.
- **Client data fetching**: TanStack Query. Fetcher: `fetcher` / mutation helper `apiFetch` from `@/lib/api`. Error messages come as `{ error: string }` JSON — show via `sonner` toast.
- **Next.js 16 specifics**: route handler `params` is a Promise → `const { id } = await params`; `cookies()` from `next/headers` is async.
- **UI rules**: shadcn/ui components from `@/components/ui`; warm amber/stone theme via CSS vars (bg-primary etc. already themed); NO blue/indigo accents; responsive (tablet-first for POS/KDS: big touch targets ≥ 44px); long lists `max-h-… overflow-y-auto rms-scroll`; cards `p-4`/`p-6`; toasts via `sonner`.
- **Do NOT**: create pages/routes other than API routes under `src/app/api/**`; do NOT touch `src/app/page.tsx` (owner: main agent); do NOT modify `prisma/schema.prisma`; do NOT install packages; do NOT write tests.
- Money is stored as Float; compare with tolerance `MONEY_EPSILON = 0.02`.

## Database (Task 0 — DONE, pushed + seeded)

Models: User(email,passwordHash,name,role[admin|waiter|kitchen],pin,active) · Category(name,displayOrder,active) · Product(name,categoryId,price,cost,isStockable,isSellable,sku,imageUrl,active,lowStockThreshold,stock) · FloorPlan(name,active) · RestaurantTable(floorPlanId,name,capacity,positionX[%],positionY[%],status[free|occupied|reserved],active) · Order(tableId?,userId,status[open|paid|cancelled],subtotalAmount,totalAmount,discountAmount,taxAmount,createdAt,closedAt) · OrderItem(orderId,productId,quantity,unitPrice,notes,course[starter|main|dessert|drink],status[new|preparing|ready|served]) · Payment(orderId,method[cash|card|other],amount,reference) · RecipeComponent(productId(dish),ingredientId,quantity, unique[productId,ingredientId]) · InventoryTransaction(productId,quantityChange,reason[sale|purchase|adjustment|waste],orderId).

Seeded: users (admin@rms.com/admin123 PIN 1234 · waiter@rms.com/waiter123 PIN 1111 · kitchen@rms.com/kitchen123 PIN 2222), 5 categories, 18 sellable dishes, 18 ingredients w/ stock, 35 recipe components, 2 floor plans (Main Hall 9 tables, Terrace 3 tables), ~35 paid historical orders over 7 days.

## Order money model

- subtotal = Σ(item.quantity × unitPrice)
- discount (absolute EGP, 0 ≤ discount ≤ subtotal)
- tax = (subtotal − discount) × TAX_RATE
- total = subtotal − discount + tax
- paidAmount = Σ payments; remaining = total − paidAmount
- Order auto-closes (status='paid', closedAt, inventory deducted, table freed) when paidAmount ≥ total − 0.02.

## API contracts (response shapes are BINDING)

All endpoints return JSON; errors: `{ "error": string }` with 4xx/5xx. Auth via httpOnly cookie (login sets it). Role gates noted per endpoint.

### Auth
- `POST /api/auth/login` body `{ email?, password?, pin? }` (pin OR email+password) → `{ user: SessionUser }` + sets cookie. 401 on bad creds.
- `POST /api/auth/logout` → `{ ok: true }`
- `GET /api/auth/me` → `{ user: SessionUser }` or 401.

### Users (admin)
- `GET /api/users` → `{ users: (SessionUser & {active:boolean; pin:string|null; createdAt:string})[] }` (no passwordHash)
- `POST /api/users` `{ name,email,password,role,pin? }` → `{ user }`
- `PUT /api/users/[id]` `{ name?,role?,pin?,active?,password? }` → `{ user }`

### Categories (read: any authed; write: admin)
- `GET /api/categories?all=1` → `{ categories: Category[] }` (default active only; `all=1` includes inactive; each has productCount)
- `POST /api/categories` `{ name, displayOrder? }` → `{ category }`
- `PUT /api/categories/[id]` `{ name?, displayOrder?, active? }` → `{ category }`
- `DELETE /api/categories/[id]` → soft delete (active=false) `{ ok:true }`

### Products (read: any authed; write: admin)
- `GET /api/products?category=<id>&all=1&sellable=1&stockable=1&search=<q>` → `{ products: Product[] }` (includes `category:{id,name}|null`; default: active only)
- `POST /api/products` `{ name, categoryId?, price, cost, isStockable?, isSellable?, sku?, imageUrl?, lowStockThreshold?, stock? }` → `{ product }` (initial stock ⇒ 'purchase' inventory transaction if stockable)
- `PUT /api/products/[id]` same fields (stock only via inventory adjust; price/cost updates allowed) → `{ product }`
- `DELETE /api/products/[id]` soft delete → `{ ok:true }`

### Floor plans & tables
- `GET /api/floorplans` → `{ floorPlans: FloorPlan[] }` (each with tables incl. polling extras: openOrderId, openOrderTotal, openOrderItemCount, openOrderSince)
- `POST /api/floorplans` `{ name }` (admin) → `{ floorPlan }`
- `PUT /api/floorplans/[id]` `{ name?, active? }` (admin) → `{ floorPlan }`
- `POST /api/tables` (admin) `{ floorPlanId, name, capacity?, positionX?, positionY? }` → `{ table }`
- `PUT /api/tables/[id]` `{ name?, capacity?, positionX?, positionY?, status?(free|reserved only), active? }` (admin) → `{ table }`
- `GET /api/tables/status` (any authed) → `{ tables: RestaurantTable[] }` with polling extras — optimized for POS polling (3s)

### Orders (waiter/admin)
- `POST /api/orders` `{ tableId?: number|null, items: [{productId, quantity, notes?, course?}] }` → `{ order: Order }` (validates stock; sets table occupied; items status='new')
- `GET /api/orders?status=open` → `{ orders: Order[] }` (with items, payments, table, paidAmount, remainingAmount; sorted createdAt asc)
- `GET /api/orders/[id]` → `{ order: Order }`
- `PUT /api/orders/[id]` `{ addItems?: [{productId,quantity,notes?,course?}], removeItemIds?: number[], updateItems?: [{id,quantity,notes?,course?}], discountAmount?: number }` → `{ order }` (recompute totals; stock validation on addItems; removed items deleted)
- `POST /api/orders/[id]/payments` (waiter/admin) `{ payments: [{method, amount, reference?}] }` → `{ order, paidAmount, remaining, closed:boolean }` — rejects overpay (sum > remaining + 0.02 → 400). If fully paid → auto close (status paid + closedAt + inventory deduction + table freed) and `closed:true`.
- `POST /api/orders/[id]/close` (waiter/admin) → only succeeds if remaining ≤ 0.02 → `{ order, closed:true }`; else 400.
- `POST /api/orders/[id]/cancel` (admin or creating waiter) → `{ order }` (status cancelled, table freed, NO inventory deduction)
- `PUT /api/order-items/[id]` (kitchen/waiter/admin) `{ status?: 'new'|'preparing'|'ready'|'served', notes?, quantity? }` → `{ item }`

### Inventory (read: waiter/admin; adjust: admin)
- `GET /api/inventory` → `{ items: InventoryItem[] }` (stockable products: stock, cost, value=cost×stock, isLow)
- `GET /api/inventory/low-stock` → `{ items: InventoryItem[] }`
- `GET /api/inventory/transactions?limit=100` → `{ transactions: InventoryTransaction[] }` (with product name, desc by date)
- `POST /api/inventory/adjust` (admin) `{ productId, quantityChange (±), reason('purchase'|'adjustment'|'waste'), note? }` → `{ product }` (reject stock < 0; updates product.stock + transaction)

### Recipes (admin)
- `GET /api/recipes?productId=<id>` → `{ components: RecipeComponent[] }` (with ingredient info) — NOTE: productId is a QUERY PARAM (path segment conflict with DELETE [id])
- `POST /api/recipes` `{ productId, ingredientId, quantity }` → upsert on (productId,ingredientId) → `{ component }`
- `DELETE /api/recipes/[id]` (id = componentId) → `{ ok: true }`

### Reports (admin)
- `GET /api/reports/sales?from=YYYY-MM-DD&to=YYYY-MM-DD` (inclusive; to end-of-day) → `SalesReport`: `{ totalRevenue, totalOrders, avgOrderValue, byMethod[], topProducts[](top 8), byCategory[], byDay[], byHour[] }` (paid orders only; revenue = Σ totalAmount; from defaults to 30 days ago, to defaults to today)
- `GET /api/reports/inventory-value` → `{ totalValue, itemCount, lowStockCount }`

## Frontend component contracts (files + exports are BINDING)

All view components are default exports, self-contained (fetch own data), take no props unless noted. Toasts: `import { toast } from 'sonner'` (main agent mounts the Sonner Toaster in page.tsx).

- `src/components/auth/login-view.tsx` → `LoginView({ onLogin: () => void })` — email/password + PIN quick login.
- `src/components/app-navbar.tsx` → `AppNavbar({ user: SessionUser, view: string, onNavigate: (view: string) => void, onLogout: () => void })` — top bar, module tabs (role-aware: waiter → POS; kitchen → Kitchen; admin → all incl. Admin dropdown).
- `src/components/admin/dashboard-view.tsx` → `DashboardView({ onNavigate }: { onNavigate?: (view: string) => void })` — KPIs (today revenue/orders, open orders, low stock), 7-day sales bar chart (recharts), top products, quick links using onNavigate.
- `src/components/pos/pos-view.tsx` → `PosView()` — table map → order screen → payment (split by equal/items/custom) → receipt.
- `src/components/kitchen/kitchen-view.tsx` → `KitchenView()` — dark KDS, polls open orders 3s, item status buttons.
- `src/components/admin/floorplans-view.tsx` → `FloorPlansView()` — floor plan manager with draggable tables.
- `src/components/admin/products-view.tsx` → `ProductsView()`
- `src/components/admin/categories-view.tsx` → `CategoriesView()`
- `src/components/admin/users-view.tsx` → `UsersView()`
- `src/components/admin/inventory-view.tsx` → `InventoryView()`
- `src/components/admin/recipes-view.tsx` → `RecipesView()`
- `src/components/admin/reports-view.tsx` → `ReportsView()`

Main agent owns `src/app/page.tsx`: view state machine (`login` → role default view; views: `pos`, `kitchen`, `dashboard`, `products`, `categories`, `floorplans`, `inventory`, `recipes`, `reports`, `users`) + TanStack `QueryClientProvider` + `Toaster`.

Task ID: 1-c
Agent: general-purpose (backend inventory/recipes/reports)
Task: Inventory, recipes and reports API routes (8 endpoints) per binding contracts

Work Log:
- Created `src/app/api/inventory/route.ts` (GET, waiter/admin): stockable+active products → InventoryItem[] (value=round2(cost×stock), isLow=stock≤threshold), sorted isLow desc then name asc (stable JS sort over Prisma name-asc).
- Created `src/app/api/inventory/low-stock/route.ts` (GET, waiter/admin): same mapping, JS-filtered to stock ≤ lowStockThreshold (Prisma can't compare column-to-column), name asc.
- Created `src/app/api/inventory/transactions/route.ts` (GET, waiter/admin): `?limit=` int (400 on non-integer, clamped to 1-500, default 100), include product {id,name}, createdAt desc, Dates → ISO strings.
- Created `src/app/api/inventory/adjust/route.ts` (POST, admin): validates productId/quantityChange≠0/reason∈{purchase,adjustment,waste} ('sale' rejected 400); product must exist+active+isStockable (400); newStock<0 → 400 `Insufficient stock: cannot reduce below zero (current ${stock})`; $transaction: update stock + create InventoryTransaction with reason `${reason} (${note})` when note present; returns full product incl. category.
- Created `src/app/api/recipes/route.ts` (GET+POST, admin): GET `?productId=` (400 if missing/non-int) → components ordered by ingredient name asc, ingredient = full product incl. stock/category, Dates serialized; POST upserts on unique (productId,ingredientId) after validating qty>0, dish sellable ('Selected dish is not a sellable product'), ingredient stockable ('Selected ingredient is not stockable'), dish≠ingredient.
- Created `src/app/api/recipes/[id]/route.ts` (DELETE, admin): `await ctx.params` (Next 16 Promise params), 404 if missing → `{ ok: true }`.
- Created `src/app/api/reports/sales/route.ts` (GET, admin): from/to parsed as LOCAL dates via new Date(y,m-1,d) + round-trip validation (400 on garbage like 2026-02-31), from>to → 400; defaults from=30 days ago, to=today end-of-day (23:59:59.999); paid orders w/ items(product+category)+payments aggregated in JS → SalesReport (byMethod amount desc; topProducts revenue desc top 8; byCategory revenue desc with 'Uncategorized' fallback; byDay zero-filled continuous local dates; byHour zero-filled 0-23 from local hour; money round2).
- Created `src/app/api/reports/inventory-value/route.ts` (GET, admin): totalValue=round2(Σcost×stock), itemCount, lowStockCount over stockable+active products.
- Verified live on :3000 with curl as admin/waiter/kitchen: all shapes match types, role gates (waiter 403 on reports/recipes/adjust, kitchen 403 on inventory read), upsert keeps component count, DELETE then 404, sales Sep1-2 cross-checked against direct DB query (8196.6 / 12 orders exact match), byDay continuity + byHour 24 entries verified, ΣbyDay/ΣbyHour == totalRevenue.
- Test side-effects reverted: Coffee Beans stock restored to seed 2.5, Olive Oil to 6, recipe pair (20,19) quantity restored to 0.02, created-then-deleted component cleaned up. NOTE: mid-test the parallel users-agent left seeded admin (id 1) `active:false` which broke logins; I re-activated it via direct DB update to continue testing (seed state restored).
- `bunx tsc --noEmit`: no errors in my files (only pre-existing infra error `TS2688 Cannot find type definition file for 'bcryptjs'` from lib/auth.ts, owned by main). `bun run lint`: no errors in my files (1 error in src/lib/orders.ts, owned by agent 1-b). dev.log: zero 500s from my routes.

Stage Summary:
- Endpoints live & verified: GET /api/inventory, GET /api/inventory/low-stock, GET /api/inventory/transactions?limit=, POST /api/inventory/adjust, GET+POST /api/recipes, DELETE /api/recipes/[id], GET /api/reports/sales?from&to, GET /api/reports/inventory-value — all response shapes match @/lib/types exactly (extra `createdAt` ISO field added to serialized products/components; additive, type-compatible).
- Decisions: transactions limit clamps out-of-range ints to [1,500] (400 only for non-integer garbage); low-stock filter done in JS (Prisma lacks column-to-column compare); adjust stores raw stock sum (no rounding) but checks newStock<0 pre-write; recipes GET requires integer productId query param; dish/ingredient not-found reported via the spec's 400 messages ('not sellable'/'not stockable').
- Inventory/recipes/reports views (agents 2-e etc.) can now consume: /api/inventory (items with isLow first), adjust for stock ops, recipes upsert/delete, reports/sales with continuous byDay/byHour for recharts.

Task ID: 1-a
Agent: general-purpose (backend auth/users/categories/products)
Task: Auth, users, categories, products API routes (14 handlers in 9 files) per binding contracts

Work Log:
- Created `src/app/api/auth/login/route.ts` (POST): PIN path (exact match on active user; accepts string or numeric pin) takes precedence, else email (lowercased+trimmed) + bcrypt verify; empty/invalid body → 400 'Email and password or PIN required'; failure → 401 'Invalid credentials'; success → createSessionToken + setSessionCookie → `{ user: {id,email,name,role} }`.
- Created `src/app/api/auth/logout/route.ts` (POST, no auth): `{ ok: true }` + clearSessionCookie. Created `src/app/api/auth/me/route.ts` (GET): getSessionUser → `{ user }` or 401.
- Created `src/app/api/users/route.ts`: GET (admin) all users createdAt asc, safe select (id,email,name,role,pin,active,createdAt — NEVER passwordHash); POST (admin) validates name/email non-empty, password ≥ 4 chars, role ∈ ROLES, pin 3-8 digits (optional; ''/null clears), email stored lowercased+trimmed, P2002 → 409 'Email already exists'.
- Created `src/app/api/users/[id]/route.ts` (PUT, admin): partial {name,role,pin,active,password}; password re-hashed only when non-empty string; self-deactivate guard → 400 'You cannot deactivate your own account'; 404 if user missing; 400 on non-integer id.
- Created `src/app/api/categories/route.ts`: GET (any authed) default active-only, `?all=1` includes inactive, orderBy displayOrder asc then id asc, productCount = ACTIVE products per category (filtered `_count` include); POST (admin) name required, displayOrder int ≥ 0 default 0. `[id]/route.ts`: PUT partial + DELETE soft (active=false) → `{ok:true}`; both 404 on missing.
- Created `src/app/api/products/route.ts`: GET (any authed) filters `category`/`all=1`/`sellable=1`/`stockable=1`/`search` (name OR sku contains — SQLite LIKE is ASCII case-insensitive), include `category {id,name}`, orderBy category.displayOrder asc then name asc (Prisma relation orderBy / LEFT JOIN); POST (admin) validates name/price/cost/lowStockThreshold/stock ≥ 0 (price/cost default 0), isStockable default false / isSellable default true, sku/imageUrl nullable, categoryId verified to exist (400 'Category not found'); initial stock on stockable product atomically creates InventoryTransaction {quantityChange: stock, reason: 'purchase'} via nested create. `[id]/route.ts`: PUT same fields partial — `stock` in body is DELIBERATELY IGNORED (verified: stock stayed 50 when 999 sent); categoryId null clears; DELETE soft; 404s on missing.
- Verified live on :3000 with curl (admin/waiter/kitchen jars): login by email/pin/numeric-pin, pin precedence when both sent, bad creds 401, empty body 400, me 200/401, logout clears cookie; users CRUD + dup email 409 + validation 400s + self-deactivate 400 + deactivated user login 401; categories CRUD + productCount (4/6/4/5, inactive 'Ingredients' 19 with all=1) + waiter/kitchen 403 on writes; products GET filters (category=1 → 4 items, sellable=1 → 19, stockable=1 → 21, case-insensitive search on name+sku, combined filters) + POST/PUT/DELETE + purchase transaction verified in DB + 403s for non-admin writes. All test rows deleted afterwards (users 3, categories 5, products 38 — seed state).
- ⚠️ FOUND + WORKED AROUND a bug in `src/lib/auth.ts` (owner: main agent): `getSessionUser` returns the DB row `{id,email,name,role}` although typed `SessionPayload {userId,...}` → `requireAuth(...).userId` is `undefined` AT RUNTIME. It silently defeated my self-deactivate guard mid-test (admin id 1 got deactivated; 1-c re-activated it via DB — thanks). My users/[id] route now reads the id defensively (`userId ?? id`) so it works with either shape. **ALL agents using session.userId (e.g. 1-b orders 'creating waiter' cancel rights) must do the same until main fixes getSessionUser** — suggested fix: return `{ userId: user.id, email, name, role }`.
- `bunx tsc --noEmit`: no errors in my files (only pre-existing global TS2688 'Cannot find type definition file for bcryptjs' — @types/bcryptjs@3.0.0 is a deprecated stub with no .d.ts; suggest main agent removes that devDependency). `bun run lint` scoped to my routes: clean (remaining repo errors are in src/lib/orders.ts and src/components/pos/cart-panel.tsx — other agents). dev.log: zero `[api-error]`/500s from my routes.

Stage Summary:
- Live & verified: POST /api/auth/login, POST /api/auth/logout, GET /api/auth/me, GET+POST /api/users, PUT /api/users/[id], GET+POST /api/categories, PUT+DELETE /api/categories/[id], GET+POST /api/products, PUT+DELETE /api/products/[id]. Response shapes match the binding contracts exactly (`{user}`, `{users}`, `{category}`, `{categories}` w/ productCount, `{product}`/`{products}` w/ category {id,name}); all errors are `{ error: string }` with 400/401/403/404/409.
- Decisions: pin beats email+password when both present; emails stored/compared lowercased+trimmed; PIN accepts 3-8 digit string or number, ''/null clears; product POST price/cost default 0 when omitted (validated ≥ 0 when present); PUT ignores `stock` per contract; uncategorized products sort first (SQLite NULL ordering under relation orderBy).
- Frontend agents can consume: login-view (email+PIN), users-view, categories-view, products-view; product creation with initial stock auto-books a 'purchase' inventory transaction (visible in /api/inventory/transactions).

- Created 11 route handlers (all Next 16 `await params`, try/catch → errorResponse, money round2 at persistence): POST+GET /api/orders (waiter/admin create; any-auth list w/ status=open|paid|cancelled|all + tableId filter, createdAt asc); GET+PUT /api/orders/[id] (open-only edit: addItems w/ stock check vs existing order, removeItemIds, updateItems qty/notes/course, discountAmount absolute EGP clamped); POST /api/orders/[id]/payments (method∈PAYMENT_METHODS, amount>0, overpay → `Payment exceeds the remaining balance (EGP X.XX)`; auto-close → {order,paidAmount,remaining,closed}); POST /api/orders/[id]/close (remaining≤0.02 else 400 `Order is not fully paid yet (remaining EGP X)`); POST /api/orders/[id]/cancel (admin OR creator waiter, 403 otherwise; paid→400; cancelled→400; frees table, NO inventory changes); PUT /api/order-items/[id] (kitchen/waiter/admin, status∈ITEM_STATUSES, qty>0, returns item incl. product); POST /api/tables + PUT /api/tables/[id] (admin; status only free|reserved — occupied system-managed → 400; capacity≥1, positions 0-100); GET /api/tables/status (any authed, active tables by floorPlanId,name + polling extras); GET+POST /api/floorplans (any authed / admin; ?all=1; tables ordered by name w/ same extras); PUT /api/floorplans/[id] (admin, name/active).

PUT +Hummus → 310/43.40/353.40; PUT −SoftDrink row → 235/32.90/267.90; PUT discount 20 → tax 30.10, total 245.10. Payment cash 50 → remaining 195.10 closed:false; overpay 500 → 400; split card 100 (ref AUTH-42) + cash 95.10 → paid 245.10, remaining 0, closed:true, status paid, closedAt set, T1 freed. I did NOT touch auth.ts (out of my file scope) — added `sessionUserId()` helper in lib/orders.ts accepting both shapes; recommend fixing auth.ts to `{ userId: user.id, … }` and dropping the helper later. (2) One transient Turbopack stale-module error (`sessionUserId is not a function`) right after mid-session edits to lib/orders.ts + cancel route — resolved by forcing a real content-change recompile; not reproducible since. ## ROUND 2 — Auth fix, Odoo UI, Print Check, Transfer/Merge (in progress)

### Auth fixes (Task R2-auth — DONE by main)
- Login loop in cross-site preview iframes fixed: `setSessionCookie(res, token, req?)`/`clearSessionCookie(res, req?)` now emit `SameSite=None; Secure` when request host is non-localhost or x-forwarded-proto=https (preview gateway), `Lax` otherwise (localhost dev). Login/logout routes pass `req`.
- Bearer fallback: login response now `{ user, token }` (token additive); `src/lib/api.ts` gained `setSessionToken/clearSessionToken/getSessionToken` (localStorage `rms_session_token`) and attaches `Authorization: Bearer` to fetcher/apiFetch; `getSessionUser` reads cookie FIRST then Bearer header; login-view stores token; page.tsx clears it on logout.
- `verifySessionToken` tolerates legacy `{id}` payload tokens.
- Agents: DO NOT modify src/lib/auth.ts, src/lib/api.ts, src/app/api/auth/**, src/app/page.tsx (owner: main).

### New Order statuses
- Order.status now also 'merged' (source order of a merge). Constants: ORDER_STATUSES + ORDER_STATUS_LABELS in @/lib/constants; type updated in @/lib/types. schema.prisma comment updated (NO db push needed).

### NEW API contracts (Task 5-a — binding)
- `POST /api/orders/[id]/transfer` (waiter/admin) body `{ tableId: number }` → `{ order: Order }` — order must be open (400 `Only open orders can be transferred`); table exists+active (404/400); no OTHER open order on target (400 `Table "X" already has an open order`; target status free|reserved OK); updates order.tableId, frees old table (only if it has no other open order), occupies new table; works from takeaway (null) → table.
- `POST /api/orders/[id]/merge` (waiter/admin) body `{ sourceOrderId: number }` → `{ order: Order }` — merges SOURCE INTO target [id]; both open; not same id (400); source items+payments reparented to target (order.update on orderId); target totals recomputed (recomputeTotals semantics); source → status='merged', closedAt=now, freed table; if target is takeaway and source has table, target takes source's table; NO inventory deduction; if paid ≥ total−0.02 after merge → auto-close (paid+deduct inventory+free table) using existing closeOrderIfFullyPaid; response includes full serialized Order (items incl. product, payments, table, paidAmount, remainingAmount).

### NEW frontend contract (Task 5-b — binding)
- `src/components/pos/check-modal.tsx` (NEW): `CheckModal({ order, open, onOpenChange })` default export — customizable pre-payment bill/check: customer name input, check title, mode Full | Equal Split | By Items | Custom, live preview + thermal print (window.print). Used from payment-modal ("Print Check" button) — printing does NOT record payments; after printing the waiter returns to the payment modal to register cash/card.
- Transfer/merge UI lives in table-select.tsx (mode flows) + pos-view.tsx top bar Transfer button.
- ALL other existing component contracts UNCHANGED.

### Theme direction (Task 5-c)
- Odoo-style light theme: bg stone-100-ish #F2F2F2, white cards, Odoo plum primary #714B67, emerald success (Payment/validate), warm status colors kept. NO blue/indigo. Dark Odoo-style top navbar. KDS stays dark.

Task ID: 5-a
Agent: general-purpose (backend transfer/merge)
Task: POST /api/orders/[id]/transfer + /api/orders/[id]/merge

Work Log:
- Read worklog.md (contracts + ROUND 2), src/lib/{orders,auth,constants,types}.ts and existing routes (orders, [id]/payments, [id]/cancel, [id]) to match style.
- Created `src/app/api/orders/[id]/transfer/route.ts` (POST): requireAuth(waiter/admin) → `await params` → parseId → body `{ tableId }` (400 `tableId is required` if missing/not an int) → order 404/`Only open orders can be transferred` → target table 404 `Table not found` (exists+active) → 400 `Table "NAME" already has an open order` (other open orders only; free|reserved targets OK). In `db.$transaction`: update order.tableId, free OLD table only if no other open order references it, set NEW table 'occupied'. Responds `{ order }` via getOrderOr404 + serializeOrder. Works takeaway (tableId null) → table.
- Created `src/app/api/orders/[id]/merge/route.ts` (POST): requireAuth(waiter/admin) → body `{ sourceOrderId }` (400 `sourceOrderId is required`) → 400 `Cannot merge an order into itself` → target/source 404 (source: `Source order not found`) + 400 `Only open orders can be merged`. In `db.$transaction`: orderItem.updateMany + payment.updateMany re-parent to target; takeaway target adopts source table (target.tableId ?? source.tableId); source → status='merged', closedAt=now, tableId=null; source table freed only if no open order remains on it (else kept 'occupied'); target table ensured 'occupied'. After commit: `recomputeTotals(targetId)` (reused helper — identical semantics, keeps TARGET discount clamped to new subtotal) then `closeOrderIfFullyPaid(targetId)` (auto-close + idempotent inventory deduction + table release). Responds `{ order }` (full serialized target). NO inventory deduction at merge time.
- Verified via curl (waiter pin 1111): created orders A(T5) 2×water+salad, B(T6) 3×water → transferred A T5→T2 (T5 freed, T2 occupied, order.tableId updated); transfer to occupied T6 → 400 `Table "T6" already has an open order`; transfer paid order → 400; transfer takeaway → T3 OK; missing/invalid tableId → 400; unknown order/table → 404. Merge B→A: items reparented, totals = 135+60 → subtotal 195/tax 27.30/total 222.30, source 'merged' + closedAt + detached, T6 freed, T2 occupied, source gone from GET /api/orders?status=open, water stock unchanged (30) and zero 'sale' transactions → no inventory deduction. Partial-payment merge C(10 EGP cash)→D: payment row moved, paidAmount 10/remaining 121.10. Takeaway target H adopted source G's table T3 (occupied, openOrderId=H). Auto-close edge (target overpaid-but-open via discount-after-payment): merged total 28.50 ≤ paid 108.20 → status 'paid' + closedAt + inventory deducted once (Soft Drink 22→21, salad ingredients) + both tables freed. Self-merge → 400; kitchen → 403; unauthenticated → 401; paid source/target → 400.
- CLEANUP: deleted the 8 test orders (items/payments cascade), the 4 test 'sale' inventory transactions, restored Soft Drink/Chicken Breast/Cheese/Olive Oil stocks and all 12 tables to 'free' — final state equals the pre-test snapshot (verified with a before/after Prisma diff); open orders list now empty.
- `bunx tsc --noEmit` → zero errors; `bun run lint` → my two files clean (the only 2 remaining errors are pre-existing in Task 5-b frontend files check-modal.tsx/table-select.tsx, not mine).

Stage Summary:
- Endpoints live on the already-running dev server:
  - POST /api/orders/[id]/transfer → 200 `{ order: Order }` (full ORDER_INCLUDE serialization: items incl. product, payments, table, user, paidAmount, remainingAmount). Errors: 400 `tableId is required` / `Only open orders can be transferred` / `Table "X" already has an open order`; 404 `Order not found` / `Table not found`; 401/403.
  - POST /api/orders/[id]/merge → 200 `{ order: Order }` (target after recompute + possible auto-close). Errors: 400 `sourceOrderId is required` / `Cannot merge an order into itself` / `Only open orders can be merged`; 404 `Order not found` (target) / `Source order not found` (source); 401/403.
- Decisions: (1) totals recomputed by reusing the imported `recomputeTotals` helper right after the transaction commits (guaranteed identical semantics + keeps target discount clamped) rather than duplicating math inside the tx; (2) `closeOrderIfFullyPaid` runs after the transaction (it uses `db`, cannot take `tx`) — exactly as the contract allows; (3) transfer to the order's own current table is a safe no-op (occupied kept); (4) merge source table kept 'occupied' when the target (or a third open order) adopted it.
- Deviations: none from the binding contract. Additive detail: source-order 404 message is `Source order not found` (contract only pinned the 404 status). Merge auto-close is only reachable when the target is already overpaid-but-open (e.g. discount applied after a near-full payment); for two normally-open orders the paid sums mathematically stay below the merged total − 0.02 — the guard is still implemented and verified.

### i18n system (main-owned core — usage for all frontend agents)
- `src/lib/i18n/index.tsx` exports `LanguageProvider` (mounted in page.tsx), `useI18n()` → `{ lang, isRTL, setLang, toggleLang, t }`.
- `t('common.save')`, `t('pos.sendToKitchen')`, `t('common.since', { time })` ({var} interpolation). Fallback: English value → key tail. NEVER hardcode English strings in JSX after your pass — route them through `t()` with keys in YOUR dict namespace (or reuse existing common.* keys).
- Your dict file: `{ en: { 'yourns.key': '…' }, ar: { 'yourns.key': '…' } }`, `export const <ns>Dict: DictPair`. You own ONLY your namespace file(s) — common.ts is read-only for you.
- Arabic: MSA + Egyptian restaurant terminology; keep numbers Latin; money via formatCurrency (auto ج.م); dates via formatDate/formatTime (auto-locale). See common.ts for established glossary (e.g. طاولة/سفري/إرسال إلى المطبخ/طباعة الحساب/تسجيل الحضور).
- RTL: provider sets `document.documentElement.dir`. Use Tailwind logical utilities for NEW code (`ms-*`, `me-*`, `ps-*`, `pe-*`, `text-start`, `text-end`) and add `rtl:` variants where existing physical classes break the layout. Flexbox/grid auto-flip. **Print windows**: set `<html dir="rtl" lang="ar">` in generated print HTML when lang==='ar'.

### Session / permissions (main-owned — binding)
- SessionUser: `{ id, email, name, role, permissions: string[], roleName: string|null }`. Login + /api/auth/me return it. role='custom' ⇒ permissions come from CustomRole row; roleName = display name.
- PERMISSIONS (module keys, in constants): pos, kitchen, dashboard, products, categories, floorplans, inventory, recipes, reports, users, roles, attendance, settings.
- Built-ins: admin → all; waiter → ['pos']; kitchen → ['kitchen'].
- requireAuth(req, allowed) — allowed may mix role names + permission keys; admin always passes. Existing routes already updated with permission keys (e.g. reports: ['admin','reports']).
- Frontend: page.tsx view machine maps view → permission (VIEW_PERMISSION); navbar builds tabs from permissions. Custom users land on first permitted view (priority pos > kitchen > dashboard > reports > …).

### NEW API contracts — 6-a (BINDING)
- `GET /api/settings` (any authed) → `{ settings: { restaurantName: string } }` (missing key → constant 'Saffron Table').
- `PUT /api/settings` ['admin','settings'] `{ restaurantName?: string }` (1-60 chars, trimmed, non-empty) → `{ settings }`. upsert key.
- `GET /api/shifts` (any authed) → `{ shifts: Shift[] }` (active+inactive, id asc). `POST /api/shifts` ['admin','settings'] `{ name, startTime 'HH:MM', endTime 'HH:MM' }` (validate 24h format, endTime may be < startTime = overnight) → `{ shift }`.
- `PUT /api/shifts/[id]` ['admin','settings'] `{ name?, startTime?, endTime?, active? }` → `{ shift }`. Soft delete via active=false only.
- `GET /api/roles` (any authed) → `{ roles: CustomRole[] }` (permissions as string[], userCount, active+inactive). `POST /api/roles` ['admin','roles'] `{ name (unique, 2-40 chars), permissions: string[] ⊆ PERMISSIONS }` → `{ role }`. 409 on duplicate name.
- `PUT /api/roles/[id]` ['admin','roles'] `{ name?, permissions?, active? }` → `{ role }`. Soft delete only; role with users may be deactivated (users keep roleName but lose perms until reassigned — acceptable).
- `POST /api/attendance/check-in` **PUBLIC** (no auth!) body `{ username, pin }` — rate-limited 10/5min per ip+username via checkRateLimit (`att:<ip>:<username>`), 429 with readable message. username matches email (ci) OR name (ci, trimmed); multiple name matches → 400 `Multiple users match this name — use your email`. pin must equal user's stored pin (user must be active; pin may be null → 401 `No PIN set for this user`). Open record = latest attendance where user & checkOutAt=null; if exists (regardless of day) → 400 `Already checked in at HH:MM`. Else create with checkInAt=now, lateMinutes computed vs active shifts: find shift where check-in time-of-day ∈ [start, start+8h] (wrap overnight: endTime<startTime means window start→end+24h); late = max(0, minutes(checkIn − shiftStart) − 15 grace) when in that window; else 0. Response `{ user: { id, name, role, roleLabel, roleName }, attendance: AttendanceRecord, late: boolean, lateMinutes, shift: { id, name, startTime, endTime } | null }`.
- `POST /api/attendance/check-out` **PUBLIC** + rate-limited, body `{ username, pin }` — same user match; find open record (checkOutAt null) → 400 `No open check-in found` if none; set checkOutAt=now. Response `{ user, attendance: AttendanceRecord (workedMinutes computed), workedMinutes, shift? }`.
- `GET /api/attendance?date=YYYY-MM-DD` ['admin','attendance'] (default today, local day window from midnight to midnight, validate/400 garbage, allow past 31 days) → `{ records: AttendanceRecord[] }` — each: user {id,name,role,roleName}, checkInAt, checkOutAt, lateMinutes, workedMinutes (null while open), shiftName (shift whose window contained the check-in), sorted checkInAt desc. Also `{ summary: { total, checkedIn, stillIn, lateCount, totalWorkedMinutes } }`.
- Users API edits (6-a): POST/PUT accept `role: 'admin'|'waiter'|'kitchen'|'custom'` + `roleId` (required+validated when custom: exists & active, else 400 `Role not found`; 400 when roleId sent with non-custom role). PIN validation: exactly 6 digits (`^\d{6}$`, message `PIN must be exactly 6 digits`), ''/null clears. GET /api/users rows gain `roleId`, `roleName` (resolved), `permissions` (derived). DO NOT weaken existing guards/validation.

NO inventory changes; tables unchanged (both already occupied); payments stay. Response via getOrderOr404 + serializeOrder for BOTH.

Work Log:
- Read worklog (ROUND 3 contracts + safety rules), src/lib/{auth,constants,types,db,rate-limit}.ts, prisma/schema.prisma (CustomRole/Attendance/Shift/AppSetting/User.roleId), existing users/categories/reports-sales/login routes for conventions; confirmed seed state (3 users w/ 6-digit pins, role 'Accountant', shifts Morning/Evening, setting 'Saffron Table', 0 attendance).

- Created `src/app/api/shifts/route.ts` + `shifts/[id]/route.ts`: GET (any authed) all shifts incl. inactive (id asc) as `Shift[]` (id/name/startTime/endTime/active only); POST ['admin','settings'] validates HH:MM 24h via `/^([01]\d|2[0-3]):([0-5]\d)$/` (400 `startTime/endTime must be a valid HH:MM time (24-hour)` / `... is required (HH:MM 24-hour format)`), name 1-60; endTime<startTime = overnight explicitly allowed. PUT partial (name/startTime/endTime/active) + 404 `Shift not found`; soft delete via active=false only — NO DELETE handler, no hard delete.
- Created `src/app/api/roles/route.ts` + `roles/[id]/route.ts`: GET (any authed) all CustomRoles (id asc) as `CustomRole[]` — permissions comma-split into string[], userCount = _count.users (ALL users with roleId, active+inactive), createdAt ISO; POST ['admin','roles'] name 2-40 trimmed (400 `Role name must be between 2 and 40 characters`), permissions must be array ⊆ PERMISSIONS (400 `Invalid permission: X` / `permissions must be an array`, deduped before join(',')), P2002 → 409 `Role name already exists`; PUT partial (name/permissions/active) with same validation + 404 `Role not found`. Soft delete only.
- Created `src/app/api/attendance/check-in/route.ts` (PUBLIC — no requireAuth): `checkRateLimit('att:'+clientIp+':'+username.slice(0,60), 10, 5min)` BEFORE processing → 429 `Too many attempts — try again in Xs`; resetRateLimit only on the 200 path. User match: email lowercased-trimmed (unique, wins) else exact name ci-trimmed; >1 distinct name matches → 400 `Multiple users match this name — use your email`; no match → 401 `Invalid credentials`; inactive → 401 `Invalid credentials`; pin null → 401 `No PIN set for this user`; string-compare pin mismatch → 401. Open record (checkOutAt null, any day, latest) → 400 `Already checked in at HH:MM` (local). Creates record with lateMinutes: ACTIVE shifts matched by time-of-day window — day shift [start, start+8h], overnight (end<start) start→end+24h incl. post-midnight wrap (checkIn ≤ endTime); multiple matches → prefer the LATEST-starting shift (17:00 boundary → Evening, not Morning's 8h tail); late = max(0, minutes(checkIn−shiftStart) − LATE_GRACE_MINUTES 15) when after start, wrap adds 24h. Response `{ user: {id,name,role,roleLabel,roleName}, attendance: AttendanceRecord, late, lateMinutes, shift: {id,name,startTime,endtime}|null }` — roleLabel = ROLE_LABELS for built-ins, roleName ?? 'Custom' for custom.
- Created `src/app/api/attendance/check-out/route.ts` (PUBLIC + same rate limit): same user/pin match; latest open record else 400 `No open check-in found`; sets checkOutAt=now; workedMinutes = round((now−checkIn)/60000) clamp ≥0. Response `{ user, attendance (workedMinutes + ISO dates), workedMinutes, shift (matched vs checkInAt time-of-day, same logic) }`.
- Created `src/app/api/attendance/route.ts` GET ['admin','attendance']: `?date=YYYY-MM-DD` default today, LOCAL day window [midnight, next midnight) using the reports/sales parseLocalDate pattern (regex + round-trip → 400 `Invalid date: expected YYYY-MM-DD` on garbage like 2026-02-31), future → 400 `Date cannot be in the future`, older than 31 days → 400 `Date must be within the past 31 days` (per ROUND 3 "allow past 31 days"). Records checkInAt desc with user {id,name,role,roleName}, lateMinutes, workedMinutes (null while open), shiftName (shift whose window contained the check-in time-of-day, recomputed against CURRENT active shifts — no shiftId stored). Summary `{ total, checkedIn (distinct employees), stillIn, lateCount (lateMinutes>0), totalWorkedMinutes }`.
- Edited `src/app/api/users/route.ts` + `users/[id]/route.ts` (additive only — every existing validation/guard preserved): POST/PUT now accept role 'custom' (ROLES already had it) + roleId — roleId with effective non-custom role → 400 `roleId can only be set when role is custom`; custom requires an existing+active CustomRole else 400 `Role not found`; PUT switching away from custom clears the stale roleId, switching to custom without a new roleId keeps+revalidates the existing link; PIN validation tightened to EXACTLY 6 digits `/^\d{6}$/` (message `PIN must be exactly 6 digits`; ''/null clears, numbers stringified). GET/POST/PUT rows gain `roleId`, `roleName` (resolved from roleRecord when roleId set — kept even if role inactive) + `permissions` via derivePermissions(role, roleRecord?.active ? permissions : null) — same semantics as sessions. PUT response mapping switched to Prisma.UserUncheckedUpdateInput so roleId is settable; passwordHash still never serialized.
- Helper functions (shift-window matching, user matching, HH:MM/date parsing, serialization) are duplicated per route file on purpose — I own ONLY the enumerated route files (no new lib/module files), and extra exports from route.ts files break Next's route type checks.

roles POST 'Cashier' ['pos','reports'] → permissions array + 409 duplicate + 400 invalid permission `Invalid permission: pizza` + 400 short name/non-array + waiter 403 + PUT rename/permissions + 409 rename-to-existing + 404; zero active shifts → shift null + late 0; overlapping shifts (Morning window + new Late Shift 16:00) → latest-start wins (Late Shift, late 21); custom-user check-in roleLabel = 'Cashier Prime'; attendance list: date=today records desc + summary {total 4, checkedIn 4, stillIn 0, lateCount 3} + 400 garbage/2026-02-31/future/>31-days-old + 200 at 31 days + waiter 403 + custom-with-attendance-perm 200 + no-auth 401; users: POST custom-role user (roleId 'Cashier Prime') → roleName+permissions in response → login with pin 654321 → permissions ['pos','attendance'] in login response → GET /api/users shows roleName; 400s: custom+bad roleId, custom w/o roleId, waiter+roleId, pin '1234'; PUT pin 5-digit 400 / '' clears / role custom→waiter clears roleId / back to custom roleId 1 / role-only keeps link / roleId null while custom 400; self-deactivate guard intact 400; deactivated role → user keeps roleName but permissions [] (session-consistent).

Stage Summary:
- Endpoints live & verified per ROUND 3 contracts: GET/PUT /api/settings; GET/POST /api/shifts; PUT /api/shifts/[id] (soft delete only); GET/POST /api/roles; PUT /api/roles/[id] (soft delete only); POST /api/attendance/check-in (PUBLIC); POST /api/attendance/check-out (PUBLIC); GET /api/attendance?date=; GET/POST /api/users + PUT /api/users/[id] (roleId + 6-digit PIN). All errors `{ error }` with proper statuses; guards: settings/shifts writes ['admin','settings'], roles writes ['admin','roles'], attendance list ['admin','attendance'] (permission-aware — custom users with the key pass).
- Decisions: (1) shift window = [start, start+8h] for day shifts and start→end+24h for overnight (post-midnight check-ins match while time-of-day ≤ endTime); overlapping windows resolve to the latest-starting shift; (2) attendance list recomputes shiftName from CURRENT active shifts (no shiftId column) — a shift created later can relabel history; (3) summary.checkedIn = distinct employees (records can exceed it via re-entries); (4) date window restricted to [today−31d, today] per "allow past 31 days"; (5) check-in 400 `Already checked in` / check-out 400 `No open check-in` do NOT reset the rate-limit bucket (reset only on 2xx); (6) PUT users: switching role away from custom nulls roleId, keeping data consistent with GET's roleId-based roleName resolution.
- Integration notes for main/6-e/6-d: login/check-in card (6-d) can POST /api/attendance/check-in with name OR email + 6-digit PIN — no auth, 10/5min per ip+username; RolesView tick grid → permissions array; SettingsView → /api/settings + /api/shifts (PUT active=false for delete; no DELETE verb); AttendanceView → GET /api/attendance?date= with summary + 15s poll; UsersView role Select sends role:'custom'+roleId (do NOT send roleId for built-ins — 400) and PIN inputs must be exactly 6 digits; AppSetting upsert means GET always returns a name (fallback constant).

Decisions:

Decisions:
- (1) Bilingual rendering is done at the model/render layer with bilingualLabel/bothLabels (never t()) so the paper is language-independent while every UI CONTROL (title select, tabs, pills, by-items chips, buttons, customer input) keeps following the UI language. (2) Payment-plan row labels arrive as finished UI-language strings from the payment modal (which I may not edit beyond the money summary), so the check rebuilds them bilingually by reverse-matching the four known templates (pos.fullBill/partOf/payer/paymentN) in BOTH languages with numeric var capture; unmatched labels pass through untouched. (3) Letter-spacing is applied per-span (Latin part only, only when UI is EN) so combined 'PAID · مدفوع' / 'DEFERRED · مؤجل' stamps never break Arabic joined letters. (4) Arabic secondary lines use dir="rtl" (correct bidi for parenthesised names) with alignment bound to the paper edge (left in LTR paper, right in RTL paper) both on-screen (text-left rtl:text-right) and in print (.ar text-align computed from isRTL). (5) By-items split checks compute VAT + service tax proportionally (same base × rate pattern as before) since the server only returns order-level tax amounts.

Stage Summary:

Day window = local midnight → +24h.
  - closed = orders status 'paid' AND closedAt in window → ordersClosed, covers Σguests, grossSubtotal ΣsubtotalAmount, discounts, vat ΣtaxAmount, serviceTax, netTotal ΣtotalAmount, avgCheck (rounded); byWaiter = group by userId (name from user, '—' fallback), net Σ, sorted net DESC.

  - payments in window (createdAt, include order.clientName) → paymentsByMethod (present methods only, amount DESC — same behavior as the sales report's byMethod) + paymentsTotal; deferredSettled = Σ payments whose order.clientName != null.

  · POST /api/orders/[id]/cancel → 'order.cancel' (total, table/takeaway).
  · POST /api/orders/[id]/transfer → 'order.transfer' (from table → to table).
  · POST /api/orders/[id]/merge → 'order.merge' (source # into target #, new total).
  · POST /api/orders/[id]/transfer-items → 'order.itemTransfer' ("N× Product" list moved from #X to #Y — items select extended with quantity + product name, read-only).
  · PUT /api/orders/[id] removeItemIds branch → 'order.itemDelete': snapshot doomed lines (product name + qty) BEFORE deleteMany, log only when removed.count > 0, after the PIN check passes; details "1× Fresh Mint Tea removed from order #96 (PIN verified)".   · PUT /api/settings → 'settings.update' ("Updated keys: restaurantName, restaurantNameAr" — keys only, VALUES never logged, PIN value never logged; skipped entirely when nothing changed).
  · POST /api/users → 'user.create' (target name/email + role, custom roleName; actor captured as `session` to avoid shadowing the created `user` row).

  · POST /api/roles → 'role.create' (name + permission list).

Work Log:
- Read worklog global conventions + last round entries (R5/R6), current transfer-items route, cart-panel.tsx, lib/orders (round2), lib/constants (MONEY_EPSILON=0.02), i18n dict pos.ts (verified pre-added keys pos.moveNUnits/moveQtyOf/moveQtyLabel in EN+AR), prisma schema OrderItem, types, i18n t signature.
- src/app/api/orders/[id]/transfer-items/route.ts (EDIT, backward-compatible extension):
  · New payload `{ targetOrderId, items?: [{id, quantity}] }`; legacy `itemIds` still accepted (each entry resolves to the FULL row quantity — old behavior byte-for-byte). Neither → 400 'items or itemIds is required'.
  · items validation: non-empty array, id positive integer, quantity finite > 0 (round2'd at parse), duplicate ids → 400 'Duplicate item id in items'.
  · Kept ALL existing checks (roles waiter/admin/pos, targetOrderId int>0, source≠target, both orders 'open', every requested item exists AND belongs to source). NEW: requested qty > row.quantity + MONEY_EPSILON → 400 `Cannot move more than the available quantity for item {id}`.
  · Classification: full move (qty ≥ row.qty − EPS, clamped to full) vs partial. ONE db.$transaction: fulls = single tx.orderItem.updateMany re-parent; partials = per entry tx.orderItem.create on target (orderId, productId, quantity=movedQty, unitPrice, notes, course, status copied as-is) + tx.orderItem.update source row to round2(row.qty − movedQty) (>0 guaranteed by classification).
  · recomputeTotals(source) + recomputeTotals(target) after tx, then getOrderOr404 + serializeOrder both, response `{source, target}` unchanged.
  · Audit 'order.itemTransfer' details list MOVED quantities: "1× Caesar Salad, 1× Hummus with Olive Oil moved from order #103 to order #101" (row select extended with productId/unitPrice/notes/course/status for the split).
- src/components/pos/cart-panel.tsx (EDIT, move-mode partial quantities):
  · New state moveQty Record<itemId, units>; toggle ON seeds full row quantity, OFF deletes key; enterMoveMode/exitMoveMode reset it (exit added for stale-state safety).
  · moveQtyFor(id) reads the map THROUGH live order items and clamps to the live row quantity (5s polling safe); moveUnits = round2(sum over selectedMoveIds); CTA switched to t('pos.moveNUnits', {n: moveUnits}), still disabled on moveCount===0 || isPending.
  · transferItems mutation now sends `items: selectedMoveIds.map(id => ({id, quantity: moveQtyFor(id)}))`; toast n = totalUnitsMoved (sum of vars.items quantities, round2). All invalidations kept.
  · SentItemRow restructured: wrapper div keeps border-b + move-mode click/aria-pressed/rounded-lg + selected bg-[#714B67]/10; inner flex row keeps exact previous content/classes; new props moveQty?/onMoveQty?.
  · Stepper sub-row rendered when moveMode && moveSelected && item.quantity > 1: [−] value [+] `of {qty}` — Buttons size icon h-10 w-10 (40px touch), Minus/Plus (already imported), formatQty value, t('pos.moveQtyOf') suffix, group role="group" with aria-label/title = t('pos.moveQtyLabel'), sr-only −/+ button names, decrement disabled at 1, increment disabled at row qty, onClick stopPropagation on the sub-row wrapper so stepper taps NEVER toggle selection, styling bg-[#714B67]/[0.06] rounded-b-lg, ms-7 indent (RTL-safe logical margin, no absolute positioning).
  · PIN delete flow, discount dialog, draft rows, everything else untouched.

Verification:
- bunx tsc --noEmit → 0 errors; bun run lint → 0 findings.
- API E2E via curl (admin@rms.com — the seed creds from prisma/seed.ts; the admin@saffron.test/Admin#2026 hint in the task brief did NOT exist in the DB): 3 takeaway test orders #101–#103 (no tables touched — floor tables T1–T9 turned out inactive, and the parallel agent's order #100 on table 16 was left alone).
  · All 9 negative cases → exact contract messages 400: targetOrderId required, items or itemIds required, items non-empty, invalid quantity, Duplicate item id in items, Cannot move more than the available quantity for item 253, same order, foreign item 999999, legacy itemIds empty.
  · Partial 1-of-3 A→B: source row 3→2 (subtotal 285→190, total 239.4), target gained NEW row qty 1 @95 (subtotal 65→160, total 201.6) ✓.
  · Full move via new format qty=full (row re-parented, same id) ✓; legacy itemIds full move ✓ (backward compat).
  · Mixed request (partial 1-of-3 + full 1-of-1 in ONE transaction): source keeps 2, target gains new row + re-parented row, subtotals 130/350, totals 163.8/441 exact ✓.
  · Audit rows verified: "1× Caesar Salad moved from order #101 to order #102", mixed "1× Caesar Salad, 1× Hummus with Olive Oil moved from order #103 to order #101" (moved qty, not row qty) ✓.
  · CLEANUP: orders 101/102/103 cancelled (freeTableIfUnused n/a — takeaway), zero leftover open orders, table statuses unchanged (16 occupied by the other agent's #100, rest free), tokens/temp files removed. Audit trail rows kept (append-only history by design).
- dev.log: only 200/400 responses for my requests, 0 errors/500s.
- UI not browser-tested (per instructions — main agent E2E-verifies); stepper logic mirrored from the API-clamped values, t('pos.moveItemsDesc') keeps n = item count per updated dict text.

Decisions:
- (1) exitMoveMode also resets moveQty (contract only required enterMoveMode to) — prevents stale per-row quantities leaking into a later move session; symmetric with the existing selection reset.
- (2) moveQtyFor clamps stored quantities against the LIVE row quantity at read time (belt-and-braces on top of the stepper clamp) so a polling refresh that shrank a row can never send an over-quantity entry; the API 400s it anyway.
- (3) Toast n counts total UNITS moved (sum of entry quantities, round2) per contract, replacing the old row-count semantics.
- (4) Stepper icon buttons get sr-only −/+ names (no suitable i18n key exists; avoids inventing dict keys I'm not allowed to add) while the group itself carries the mandated aria-label/title = t('pos.moveQtyLabel').

Stage Summary:
- The "Move items" flow now supports partial quantities end-to-end: the waiter selects a sent row, optionally dials the units down with an inline [−] n [+] of {qty} stepper, and moves just those units to another open check — the API splits the row atomically (new row on target, remainder on source, both totals recomputed), full rows still move via the old bulk re-parent, and the legacy itemIds payload keeps working. Bilingual EN/AR (pre-added keys), RTL-safe, touch-friendly, audit-logged with moved quantities. tsc 0 errors, lint 0 findings, API verified live with clean DB state.

  · Props: `active?: boolean` (default true) for page.tsx's keep-mounted-hidden cooperation.
  · New refs: draftStashRef (Map<string, DraftItem[]>), appliedNavRef (sync mirror of the current sub-hash — render-lag-proof idempotency for the popstate+hashchange double fire), suppressNavPushRef, navInFlightRef + navEpochRef (duplicate/supersede guard), navHandlerRef (latest-render handler for the single onNav subscription), prevActiveRef.
  · navPush wrapper (silent during restores; user push bumps epoch → aborts in-flight restore). stashDraft/restoreStash (key `table:{id}` / `takeaway`, pop-and-delete on restore attempt, toast pos.draftRestoredToast).
  · resetToTables = exitToFloor: stash + full reset (unchanged semantics incl. dialogs/refs/transferOrderId) + appliedNavRef=null + replaceNav('pos'). startTransfer + handlePaySuccess(closed) got the same stash + replaceNav('pos') additions (conservative, original reset lists kept byte-for-byte).
  · Pushes (user-initiated only): applySelectTable → order/{openOrderId} | table/{id} (also pops the table stash — occupied OR free, one code path); startTakeaway → takeaway (pops 'takeaway' stash); openTakeawayOrder → order/{id}.
  · onNav handler: floor → resetToTables (stash+reset+idempotent replace); order/{id} → fetch /api/orders/{id} (fetcher) → open ⇒ restoreLiveOrder (selectedTable from order.table, cache seeded ['pos-order',id], toast pos.orderRestoredToast) else cannotReopenToast + replaceNav('pos') + reset; table/{id} → imperative fetcher /api/floorplans (TableSelect's exact query/endpoint + cache setQueryData) → occupied ⇒ replaceNav to order/{id} + order restore; free ⇒ withNavSuppression(applySelectTable(table, 2)) (draft + stash pop); missing ⇒ cannotReopen; takeaway ⇒ withNavSuppression(startTakeaway()) + stash pop. Guards: claim/release epoch (duplicate same-target ignores, different target supersedes), hashStill() re-check after every await (silent abort if the user navigated mid-flight), floorplans-failure path only resets when the hash still owns the target (never clobbers another view's hash).
  · Effects: latest-handler ref assign (every render) → single onNav subscription (unsubscribes on unmount) → mount-time deep-link restore (refresh/bookmark on #/pos/order/N | table/N | takeaway) → active false→true flip re-asserts the deep hash (order/N | table/N | takeaway) after page.tsx's shallow #/pos push.
- `bun run lint` → 0 findings; `bunx tsc --noEmit` → 0 errors; dev.log → clean compile, no new 500s. Did not touch cart-panel.tsx / page.tsx / api / nav.ts / dicts / prisma; no packages; no tests; no DB mutations.

Stage Summary:
- Browser/OS back now works inside the POS: entering an order/draft screen pushes a real history entry; back returns to the floor stashing unsent items; forward re-enters the exact screen (live order restored via API with 'Reopened order #N' toast, table draft restored with 'Unsent items restored' toast, occupied-redirect table/{id}→order/{id} self-heals stale draft hashes); refresh/deep links restore the same way. All in-app exits (back button, transfer, defer, cancel, payment-close) land on #/pos via replaceNav — no history spam. In-app back and browser back behave identically and never lose unsent drafts. `active` prop lets page.tsx keep PosView mounted-hidden across view switches and re-asserts the deep hash on return. Flows traced OK: (a) table draft stash/restore, (b) back+forward order restore, (c) Kitchen→POS keeps order #N with hash #/pos/order/N, (d) in-app back adds no entries, (e) refresh on #/pos/order/N restores. Deviations from spec: idempotency checks additionally use a synchronous appliedNavRef mirror (render-lag-proof equivalent of the specified mode/activeOrderId/selectedTable checks — both are checked); restore functions also stash the current context's draft before applying (hand-edited hash jumps never lose items); occupied-table redirect reuses the order/{id} restore inline (same semantics, single claim) instead of re-dispatching.

- Created 6 API routes: GET /api/sync/status and PUT /api/sync/settings (admin/settings guard, zod-validated targetUrl ''|http(s), rotateKey, audit sync.settings); POST /api/sync/export (mode delta|full; delta advances the watermark captured BEFORE reading rows so nothing read mid-build is lost; full does not mark; audit with mode+counts); POST /api/sync/push (400 without targetUrl; builds bundle, POSTs to {target}/api/sync/import with x-rsm-sync-key + 20s AbortSignal.timeout; non-2xx/network ⇒ 502 with remote status; watermark + lastPushAt only on success so a failed push never drops rows; audit sync.push); POST /api/sync/import (dual auth: admin/settings session OR x-rsm-sync-key via crypto.timingSafeEqual; format rsm-sync/1 enforced; key-auth audits as user "system (sync)" userId 0 — audit_logs.user_id has no FK, verified via PRAGMA foreign_key_list; returns SyncImportSummary); GET /api/desktop/package?data=live|demo (force-dynamic, buffers zip, ALWAYS cleans temp, streams application/zip attachment with content-length, audit desktop.package).
- Created src/lib/windows-package.ts: mkdtemp tree with src/ prisma/ public/ + 8 root config files (NEVER-COPY excludes enforced via fs.cp filter: node_modules, .next, .git, backups, screenshots, download, db, .env, scripts, mini-services, examples, agent-ctx, worklog.md, bun.lock*, logs, tests, upload, tool-results, skills); patched package.json (dev/start → "next dev -p 3000" / "next start -p 3000", unix `| tee` pipes removed); fresh .env `DATABASE_URL=file:../db/custom.db` (schema-relative, forward slashes — verified NOT the dev machine's absolute path); db/custom.db = live VACUUM INTO snapshot (backup.ts escaping pattern) or demo = `bun x prisma db push --skip-generate` + `bun run prisma/seed.ts` against the temp file via env override (bun/prisma env vars beat .env — proven by test; live db untouched) + verified with a throwaway PrismaClient (users>0); windows/install.bat + start.bat + stop.bat + bilingual EN/AR README-WINDOWS.md (CRLF verified: 0 bare LFs); zip via info-zip (never includes its own output), >100KB sanity check, cleanup on every failure path.
- Verification (all green): lint 0 findings; tsc — 1 PRE-EXISTING error in src/components/mobile/waiter-portal.tsx (parallel frontend agent's file, actively edited during my run — NOT mine; my 8 files: 0 errors). Live curl matrix with admin session: status 200 (masked key 031c…→c2d8…9179 after rotation, pending recomputed); settings PUT clear/validate (ftp:// ⇒ 400, non-boolean ⇒ 400, empty body ⇒ 400); delta export 200 (2 audit rows + ALL 4 open orders #127/128/132/133 + their 11 items — live-state guarantee proven); SELF-IMPORT idempotency: delta bundle 0 inserted/17 updated/0 skipped, FULL bundle (1,294 rows) 0 inserted/1,294 updated/0 skipped; push without target 400; push to unreachable https://example.invalid ⇒ 502 with reason; push FULL LOOP to self (target=http://localhost:3000) ⇒ 200 ok:true, remote summary 0 inserted/1,299 updated via key auth; key rotation ⇒ old key 401, new key 200, anonymous 401, waiter 403 on all sync+package routes; garbage {format:'nope'} ⇒ 400. Windows packages: live 896,934 B + demo 830,887 B zips (headers application/zip + attachment + content-length verified), unzip -l contains src/app/page.tsx, prisma/schema.prisma+seed.ts, windows/*.bat, db/custom.db, .env, README-WINDOWS.md, public/manifest.webmanifest — 0 forbidden entries (no node_modules/.next/backups/dev.log/worklog/.git); packaged .env = relative URL; bat files pure CRLF; live snapshot integrity ok (3 users/128 orders/4 open/648 audit), demo db integrity ok (3 users incl. admin@rms.com/39 orders/48 products/2 customers, no -wal residue); live db untouched by demo generation (128 orders before AND after); no /tmp/rsm-windows-* leftovers (cleanup proven); desktop.package audit rows written.

Stage Summary:

Work Log:
- Read worklog tail (R9–R15 context), src/lib/auth.ts (requireAuth/getSessionUser/ApiError/errorResponse model), src/lib/rate-limit.ts, src/lib/constants.ts permission model (18 module permissions; waiter=pos, kitchen=kitchen, admin=all).
- Enumerated all 84 route.ts files via glob; diffed (comm) the files containing requireAuth/getSessionUser vs the full list → exactly 6 files without a session guard (api root, auth/login, auth/logout, attendance/check-in, attendance/check-out, integrations/delivery/webhook) — all read line-by-line and classified (public-by-design or key-auth); zero HOLEs.
- Read every remaining route file (orders ×8, order-items, cash-drawer ×2, reports ×5, users ×2, roles ×2, settings, audit, integrations ×2, sync ×5, admin/backup ×2, desktop/package, vision ×14, tables ×4, products ×3, categories ×2, modifier-groups ×2, recipes ×2, inventory ×4, invoices, shifts ×2, customers ×2, reservations ×3, floorplans ×2, attendance) verifying per-handler guards, permission lists, id validation, bounds, status-transition checks and IDOR handling.
- Verified secrets handling in lib/sync.ts (crypto-random 48-hex key, timingSafeEqual compare, maskSyncKey first4…last4, rotate), lib/vision.ts (rms-vision-+32hex, maskKey, rotate returns full key once, stream-URL credential rejection + masking), lib/backup.ts + admin/backup/download (strict ^custom-(auto|manual)-\d{8}-\d{6}\.db$ regex before any fs access), lib/windows-package.ts (server-generated temp paths only; $executeRawUnsafe inputs are not user-controlled and quote-escaped).
- Checked login brute-force protection (in-memory sliding window 10/5min per ip+identifier, reset on success), attendance endpoints (same scheme per ip+username), PIN flows (login quick-PIN, item-delete/manager discount PIN, employee check-in PIN).
- Checked global posture: no src/middleware.ts (verified absent), next.config.ts has no security headers/CORS, .env contains only DATABASE_URL (JWT_SECRET unset), SameSite=None cross-site cookie policy + Bearer-in-localStorage fallback.

Stage Summary:
- Verdict table: 84 route files / 107 handlers — 78 files fully session-guarded with correct permission tiers, 6 deliberate public/key-auth (login, logout, me-adjacent api root, attendance kiosk ×2, delivery webhook x-rsm-key), 2 dual-auth (sync/import session-or-key, vision/events session-or-key) → NO unauthenticated holes found; admin surfaces (users, roles, settings, backup, sync, desktop package, vision config) all correctly gated.
- Findings: 1 CRITICAL (JWT_SECRET unset → tokens signed with hardcoded 'rms-dev-secret-change-me' fallback in lib/auth.ts:11 — forgeable admin sessions on this instance AND every packaged Windows copy whose generated .env lacks JWT_SECRET); 1 HIGH (login rate-limit bucket keyed by ip+identifier where PIN logins make the identifier the guessed PIN itself → distinct-guess buckets defeat the limiter; 10^6 PIN space sprayable); 3 MEDIUM (raw deliveryWebhookKey returned to 'customers'-permission roles via GET /api/integrations; shared manager/delete PIN checkable without throttle from any waiter session; user PINs returned cleartext by GET /api/users and stored unhashed); several MEDIUM-LOW/LOW (implicit-only CSRF defense under SameSite=None, Bearer token in login body/localStorage, errorResponse leaking internal messages on 500, non-constant-time vision key compare, webhook key accepted via ?key= query param, no security headers/middleware, admin-only SSRF surface in sync push targetUrl, /api scaffold hello-world route).
- Positives: 100% guard coverage on DB-touching routes, fresh per-request user/permission reload (instant revocation), IDOR correctly handled (my-shift admin-only cross-user, order cancel creator-or-admin), airtight backup/package path validation, masked+rotatable sync/vision keys with timing-safe compare on sync, bcrypt logins, rate-limited public endpoints, PIN-gated destructive ops, pervasive audit logging, disciplined bounds/enum validation everywhere, no raw-SQL injection surface.
- Security score: 6/10 — route-level auth architecture is exemplary (would be 8.5 alone), dragged down by the forgeable default JWT secret (effective total auth bypass as deployed), the PIN-spray limiter bypass, and cleartext PIN/key exposure to semi-privileged roles. Recommended next actions: (1) fail-fast on missing JWT_SECRET + write it into the Windows package generator's .env, (2) per-IP rate limit independent of identifier for login, (3) mask the delivery webhook key, (4) drop pin from user list payloads, (5) throttle manager-PIN attempts, (6) constant-time vision key compare, (7) add security headers.

Work Log:
- Context recovery (honest): found that a previous incarnation of task 17-a had already written all 5 owned files (timestamps 11:54–12:01, DB test rows from 12:17–12:18) but died before testing/worklogging. Reviewed every file line-by-line against the contract instead of rewriting: all compliant; then executed the verification it never did. Residue from that partial run (supplier "Nile Foods Supplies" + PO-0001 received / PO-0002 cancelled-after-partial-receipt / PO-0003 cancelled) was kept as intentional demo data — every stock effect is a consistent receipt (reason 'purchase' ledger rows).
- Created src/app/api/suppliers/route.ts + [id]/route.ts: GET list with purchaseCount (count of POs) + totalPurchased (Σ receivedQuantity×unitCost over NON-cancelled POs, round2), POST create / PUT update (name required-trimmed; 404 on missing id) / DELETE with 409 while any PO references the supplier; audits supplier.create/update/delete. Guarded requireAuth(['admin','purchases']).
- Created src/app/api/purchase-orders/route.ts: GET newest-first with ?status= filter (validated enum, 400 otherwise), lines include product {id,name,nameAr,sku,cost} + supplier + createdBy name, ISO dates, round2 total/receivedTotal/outstanding; POST creates 'draft' PO with number "PO-" + zero-padded 4 (max numeric suffix + 1, computed inside the $transaction), validates supplier exists+active, non-empty lines (productId exists, quantity > 0, unitCost ≥ 0), createdById = session user, nested line creates, audit purchase.create.
- Created src/app/api/purchase-orders/[id]/route.ts: GET single (404); PATCH {confirm} draft→ordered + orderedAt; {cancel} draft|ordered→cancelled (received → 409, already-cancelled → 400); {receive} only from 'ordered' — per line newReceived = clamp(receivedQty,0,quantity) treated as the NEW CUMULATIVE total, delta = round2(newReceived − current); every positive delta writes an InventoryTransaction {reason:'purchase'} + increments product.stock + revalues product.cost to the line unitCost (last-purchase price); when all lines are within 0.001 of full → status 'received' + receivedAt, else stays 'ordered' (partial receiving). All in one $transaction; audit purchase.receive with line/unit counts. DELIBERATE DEVIATION (documented): a receivedQty BELOW the already-received level is rejected 400 ("cannot be reduced") — the contract's literal clamp would silently decrement receivedQuantity without reversing the stock ledger; rejecting protects inventory integrity, and normal cumulative flows are unaffected. Delta-0 resends (already-full lines) are idempotent no-ops.
- Replaced src/components/admin/purchases-view.tsx (1,531 lines): Tabs "Suppliers | Purchase Orders"; suppliers tab = directory card + table (name/address, phone/email, orders, lifetime purchased via formatCurrency, active badge) with mobile card list, Add/Edit Dialog (name validated, phone/email/address/notes, active Switch) and delete AlertDialog whose Delete button is disabled with an amber "has purchase orders" explanation when purchaseCount > 0 (and the 409 race surfaces as a toast); PO tab = newest-first cards (number, StatusBadge: draft=secondary/ordered=amber/received=emerald/cancelled=muted, supplier, total, expected date, createdBy, outstanding when ordered) opening a detail Dialog with the full line table (item, qty, unit cost, received x/y, line total), totals block, note, and status-appropriate actions — draft: Confirm+Cancel; ordered: Receive (per-line number inputs prefilled with outstanding, "Receive all", live "+units · EGP" footer) + Cancel; received/cancelled: read-only with timestamp badges. New PO dialog: supplier Select (active only), searchable product combobox (stock-tracked group first), qty + unit-cost (defaults to product.cost) per line, add/remove lines, live total, note, expected date (noon-anchored). TanStack Query ['suppliers']/['purchase-orders'] + invalidation of products/inventory surfaces after receipts, sonner toasts, skeletons, error cards with Retry, empty states, max-h-96 rms-scroll lists, aria-labels on icon buttons, zero blue/indigo.
- API lifecycle test (curl, admin token): supplier PUT phone→+20 100 555 1234 OK; POST supplier without name → 400; created PO-0004 draft (Rice 20 @ 30.50 + Potatoes 15 @ 13.75, total 816.25, createdBy Amina Hassan, expectedAt 2026-09-20); receive-on-draft → 400; confirm → ordered+orderedAt; stock BEFORE Rice 14.5@28 / Potatoes 24.25@15; partial receive (rice 8/20, potatoes 15/15) → stayed ordered, receivedTotal 450.25, outstanding 366, stock AFTER Rice 22.5@30.50 / Potatoes 39.25@13.75 (exact +8/+15 with cost revaluation); reduction attempt rice 8→5 → 400; second receive (rice→20 cumulative, potatoes idempotent) → status received + receivedAt, receivedTotal 816.25, outstanding 0, FINAL stock Rice 34.5 (=14.5+20 exactly, no double count on the resent full line); cancel-on-received → 409; DELETE supplier with POs → 409 ("has 4 purchase order(s)"); supplier create→delete roundtrip (0 POs) → 200; ?status=received→2 / ordered→0 / draft→0 / bogus→400; missing PO → 404; waiter token → 403 both routes; no token → 401; final aggregates purchaseCount 6, totalPurchased 4,094.25 (= PO-0001 3,278 + PO-0004 816.25 — cancelled PO-0002's 570 received correctly excluded); inventory ledger shows Rice +8/+12, Potatoes +15, Milk +12, Tomatoes +10 all reason 'purchase'.
- UI verification (agent-browser): suppliers table renders aggregates (EGP4,094.25) + aria-labelled row actions; PO tab lists 6 POs newest-first; PO-0004 detail read-only (Rice 20/20, Potatoes 15/15); PO-0006 (ordered) shows Receive + Cancel PO; Receive dialog prefilled with outstanding (24, 10) and live "Receive (+34)"; executed a REAL partial receive through the UI (Milk 12/24 + Tomatoes 10/10) → toast "Received — stock updated", API-verified stock Milk 17.92→29.92 @24→26, Tomatoes 5.96→15.96 @12→14.5, PO stays ordered with outstanding 312; New PO dialog: supplier Select, product combobox (stock-tracked first), unit cost autofill (Flour → 18), live total, disabled remove-line at 1 row; delete-blocked dialog shows amber explanation + disabled Delete; Arabic RTL fully translated (الموردون وأوامر الشراء، استلام) with dir=rtl; 390px mobile zero horizontal overflow on both tabs; zero console/page errors; screenshots /tmp/r17a-*.png.

- Purchasing module complete and proven: Odoo-style supplier directory + PO lifecycle (draft → ordered → partially/fully received, cancel terminal) where receiving posts InventoryTransaction 'purchase' rows, increments stock by exact deltas and revalues cost to last purchase price — all numbers verified end-to-end via API and through the real UI (a browser-driven partial receipt landed +22 units of stock to the penny).

- Payroll route (reviewed, kept): requireAuth ['admin','payroll']; month param parsed as LOCAL first-of-month (round-trip validated, garbage → 400, default = current month); window = first-of-month → first-of-next-month; sessions filtered exactly per contract (checkInAt < windowEnd AND (open OR checkOutAt > windowStart)); hours = (checkOutAt ?? min(now, windowEnd)) − max(checkInAt, windowStart), clamped ≥ 0, round2 per user; lateMinutes summed only for sessions STARTING inside the window; grossPay = round2(hours × rate); sort grossPay desc; users without rate/sessions omitted; from/to = inclusive first/last day of month. Matches PayrollReport/PayrollLine in types.ts exactly.
- Forecast route (reviewed, kept — clean implementation, nothing salvaged from the purged draft): requireAuth ['admin','reports']; history = last 28 COMPLETE local days excluding today (zero-filled, actual = round2(Σ paid-order totalAmount), dayLabel en-US short weekday, projected null); projection = today + 6 days at the SAME-WEEKDAY average of history days WITH data (a weekday with no data falls back to avgDaily — this reading is the only one that keeps the fallback reachable since a 28-day window always covers 4 of each weekday); avgDaily = round2(Σ actual / days with data); projectedWeekTotal = round2(Σ projections). Matches ForecastReport/ForecastDay exactly.
- payroll-view fixes: (1) invalid-rate toast was echoing the field label (t('r17.payroll.rateLabel')) — replaced with a bilingual component-local literal 'Enter a valid rate (0 or more)' / 'أدخل أجرًا صحيحًا (0 أو أكثر)' (dict files are read-only for me); (2) pulled `lang` from useI18n. New POST /api/auth/person re-issues the session token with the selected person (404 on cross-account person attempts — verified).

UI: stepper block now leads with "Available: N", a direct numeric input (local text state so typing is never fought by clamping) and an "All" quick-action chip (Move All); parent gains setMoveQtyExact clamped 1..available.
- CHECK ATTRIBUTION: new POST /api/orders/[id]/check-issue (stamps the session PERSON + timestamp only while unstamped and only for person-level sessions — an account-level session never burns the stamp slot; every presentation audited); payments stamp too when unset; serializeOrder exposes checkIssuedByPerson/checkIssuedAt; check paper + on-screen modal show "Waiter: <person>" (stamped issuer → current session person → account fallback) and an "Issued: <datetime>" row; the Print action fires the stamp fire-and-forget (never blocks printing).

New persons CRUD under /api/users/[id]/persons (+[personId]) — create/rename/deactivate (NO delete: history references persons; deactivation keeps attribution readable; duplicate-name guard 409). users APIs now return each account's people roster.

No new analytics tables — 100% derived from existing data.

- 21-a SNAPSHOT AUTOMATION (the resilience answer to the two 2026-09-17 resets):
  - scripts/snapshot.ts + src/lib/db-snapshot.ts: single portable engine (pure Prisma + node:fs — runs in the node dev server, bun CLI, and the Windows package): integrity_check + foreign_key_check gates (a corrupt snapshot can NEVER replace a good recovery point) → VACUUM INTO backups/auto/custom-<stamp>.db → verify the snapshot itself → atomic (tmp+rename) refresh of download/rsm-platform-database.db (the file that survived both resets while db/ and backups/ were wiped) → manifest refresh (live table counts) → rotation (keep 30). Short-lived PrismaClient per snapshot so client regeneration can never strand it (the R19 incident).
  - package.json: db:snapshot (manual CLI) + db:watch (standalone watcher, for real deployment environments).
  - SANDBOX DISCOVERY (extensive, honest): background processes spawned from tool calls are killed at call end — nohup, setsid+disown, AND listening sockets all get swept (heartbeat probes proved it: processes die before their first 5-second tick; a subagent's spawns die identically; no cron/systemd/sudo available; PID 1 is tini with no supervisor). BUT the Next.js server process (platform-managed) survives. SOLUTION: run the watcher INSIDE the server — src/instrumentation.ts (register() hook, future boots) + src/lib/snapshot-watch-init.ts (lazy side-effect import wired into the root layout AND /api/auth/me — guarantees the watcher lives even in a server instance that predates instrumentation.ts), globalThis-guarded, 10-min interval, stat-mtime change detection, never throws. VERIFIED LIVE: "[snapshot-watcher] in-app watcher started" in dev.log; auto-watch snapshots #3 (22:34) and #4 (23:27) taken by the server process across restarts.
  - .zscripts/ensure-dev.sh: idempotent dev-server restarter (bridge utility for work sessions; documented inside the script).
- 21-b SYNC ENGINE EXPANSION (R17's top documented residual — "the 7 new R17 tables are NOT yet in sync bundles"):
  - Inspection first (Explore agent report mapped all 9 registration sites + bundle semantics + per-model readiness). Two latent bugs found by the inspection: (1) normalizeOrder/normalizeAuditLog SILENTLY DROPPED the R19 person-attribution fields on every import; (2) an order's final paid/cancelled state NEVER rode a delta (createdAt-only watermark + open-order carve-out = the close transition was invisible to offline instances — a pre-R21 hole in the original design).
  - SCHEMA (additive only, db push --skip-generate then separate prisma generate to avoid the R19 client-regeneration race): updatedAt DateTime? @updatedAt on Order, Supplier, PurchaseOrder, PurchaseOrderItem (+createdAt), StockCount, StockCountLine (+createdAt), Promotion, Person, CustomRole. Line tables had NO timestamps at all. Existing rows NULL → treated as old → ride full bundles; all future writes tracked. No column changed, no table dropped; 35 tables, integrity ok.
  - src/lib/sync.ts: expanded all 9 registration sites — computePendingCounts + buildSyncBundle (mutatedWhere OR[createdAt,updatedAt] for mutating tables; orders keep the open-order carve-out AND gain updatedAt so close transitions propagate; persons + customRoles are tiny reference tables that ride EVERY bundle in full so order.checkIssuedByPersonId and role references never dangle), 9 new row types + defensive normalizers (invalid FK → row skipped, never a crash), emptyCounts + importSyncBundle in strict FK order (customRoles → persons BEFORE orders), order import gains an FK-fallback (dangling check issuer → order syncs with attribution nulled instead of being lost). FIXED normalizeOrder (checkIssuedByPersonId/checkIssuedAt/updatedAt) + normalizeAuditLog (personId/personName).

Diagnosed via audit-vs-DB comparison; a one-character content touch forced recompile and everything works. Documented as a dev-mode hazard: after editing server libs, verify behavior, not just compilation.

Header comment documents the full release flow (bump version → tag desktop-vX.Y.Z → push → apps self-update).

Stage Summary:
- Files created (8): desktop/package.json, desktop/main.js, desktop/preload.js, desktop/prepare-server.mjs, desktop/README.md, desktop/resources/icon.png, .github/workflows/desktop-release.yml, README.md (root). Files modified (2): .gitignore (appended desktop block), eslint.config.mjs (added desktop/** ignore — see deviation above).
- The Windows app is a thin, robust shell around the existing platform: same standalone Next server, same SQLite seed (download/rsm-platform-database.db staged by CI), per-PC data + secrets under %APPDATA%/rsm-desktop, and electron-updater pointing at the public repo's Releases (NSIS channel auto-updates; portable exe is a download-only convenience).
- CI risks (specific, for the lead): (1) First-run unknown — the full chain (bun install on windows-latest → bunx next build with Turbopack → prepare-server → electron-builder NSIS publish) has never executed; watch the first desktop-v* run. (2) Electron 31 embeds Node 20.14/20.15 — Next 16's floor is 20.9, satisfied, but if the standalone runtime trips on a newer Node API the fix is bumping electron ^31.7.0 → ^32/^33 in desktop/package.json (no code change). (3) `--frozen-lockfile` + sharp/@prisma optional native deps on Windows should resolve from bun.lock, but a bun/Windows quirk would surface at install time. (4) 40-min timeout is generous but Turbopack on a 2-core windows runner is the slowest step. (5) workflow_dispatch without a tag publishes for desktop/package.json's version — if that release already exists the publish step can conflict; tag pushes are the canonical flow. (6) Releases created with the per-run GITHUB_TOKEN do not trigger other workflows (by GitHub design — harmless here). (7) SmartScreen will always warn on the unsigned exe — documented in the README ("More info → Run anyway"); a code-signing cert can be added later via standard CSC_* secrets without config changes.
- Release runbook (for the lead): commit these files → push → tag desktop-v1.0.0 → push tag → CI publishes Setup + Portable + latest.yml; installed apps then update themselves on the 30-min check + next quit. Keep desktop/package.json "version" and the tag suffix in sync (artifacts/latest.yml are versioned from package.json).
